Water-fill implementation:
def _waterfill_C(C, L, T):
  C=np.asarray(C,float).copy()
  C[~finite]=0; C[:]=max(C,0)
  if C.sum()==0: return np.full(L,1/L)
  if T <= 1/L: return ones? Actually if T=1/L, return flat.
  # cap iteratively
  for _ in range(5):
    cap = np.minimum(C, T)
    if cap.sum() >= 1:
       return cap/cap.sum()
    # need add residual to entries below T
    residual = 1 - cap.sum()
    room = np.maximum(0, T - cap)
    cap[room>0] += residual * (room[room>0] / room.sum())
  # If still sum<1 (T too small), scale? If N*T<1, infeasible; but we ensure T>=1/L.
  return cap/cap.sum()

But C has L entries (2n-1), some zero outside if N>L? Actually C only length L; if N>L zero outside not in waterfill. Good. Feasibility needs length L. T>=1/L.

def _accel_lse(a, n, stop_time, seed, initial_beta=None):
  best_a=a, best_score=fft_score(a)
  rng=default_rng(seed)
  theta? Use z=log(a+aeps)-log(a+eps).mean? Simpler use z=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-12,1e12)), z-=z.mean(). But if a zeros, use log eps.
  beta = int(max(50, n*1.5))? Since b ~1/n, beta*n ~? Let b_max~0.004, beta=1000 => beta*b~4. Good. beta = max(100, int(2000000? no). Use beta = max(50.0, 2.0*n)?? For n=192 beta=384 -> max product ~1.5, soft. Need sharper: beta=20*n? 3840 product~15. Maybe large causes underflow but logsumexp stable. Gradient dominated by peak. Start beta=10*n, then increase by 1.2 every 50 iterations if not improving.
  z maybe length n-1? Use full z with mean subtract.
  momentum=0.7, prev_z? Use Nesterov: z_curr, z_prev.
  step = 0.1? Need backtracking.
  For iter:
    if time > stop: break
    y = z_curr + momentum*(z_curr - z_prev)  (if first, y=z_curr)
    y = clip(y,-10,10)
    a_y = softmax(y)
    b=conv(a_y)
    score=2n max b (normalized)
    if score<best: update best (exact? only FFT)
    # compute LSE and gradient
    beta local
    p = softmax(beta*b) with threshold
    h = apply weighted lags to a_y
    grad = 2*beta*a_y*(h - dot(a_y,h))
    grad[~finite]=0
    # backtracking line search in z: try step factors [1,0.5,0.25...] up to 5, maybe also increase? Since objective nonconvex, use Armijo? Need loss value. Compute loss current = LSE(beta,b) for a_y. For candidate z = y - step*grad. But if no decrease, maybe try small random step.
    loss_y = beta*LSE? Actually loss=U (logsumexp beta b)
    for factor in (step, 0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625):
       zn = y - factor*grad
       zn -= zn.mean(); clip
       a_n=softmax(zn)
       b_n=conv
       U_n = LSE(beta,b_n)
       if U_n < U_y - 1e-12 or (U_n < U_y and exact score <= old_score*1.02): accept
    if not accept: perturb z, reduce step, reset momentum, maybe increase beta.
    momentum adapt.
  Return best.

So L-BFGS objective:
  def obj(theta, g=None):
    z = np.empty(n); z[:-1]=theta; z[-1]=0
    a = softmax(z) (stabilized)
    b = conv_full(a)
    U = lse(beta,b)
    if g is None return U
    p = softmax(beta*b) (same function)
    h = apply_p(p,a,n)
    S = dot(a,h)
    gradz = 2*beta*a*(h-S)
    g[:] = gradz[:-1]
    return U

Function _lp_blend(a, n, stop_time, max_calls=20):
  best=a, score=fft_score(a)
  for call in range(max_calls):
    if time>stop break
    a=best; a_pos=np.maximum(a,1e-9); a_pos/=sum
    b=conv(a_pos); M=max(b)*1.001
    build A
    try linprog
    if not success or sum<=0 break
    g=x; g=np.maximum(g,0); sg=g.sum(); if finite and >0: g/=sg
    # Take line search blend
    alphas = [0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.2? but sum mix]
    for alpha in sorted: 
       cand = (1-alpha)*a_pos + alpha*g
       # maybe if alpha>1 extrapolate: cand = a_pos + alpha*(g-a_pos), clip nonneg normalize
       cand=_reproject(cand)
       sc=fft_score(cand); exact if close
       if sc<best_score: update best; maybe use best=a_pos=?
    if no improvement for 5 calls break
  return best, score

_fourier_iterations(a, stop_time, target_score, seed):

  # choose T schedule
  base_t = target_score/(2*n) if target>0 else current_max
  cur_t = max(base_t*1.2, current_max*0.9, 1.02/L)
  # min_t = max(1.01/L, base_t*0.98)
  For iter up to time:
    C=ifft(H*H,N).real
    C=C[:L]
    if N>L: C[L:]=0
    C=0.5*(C+C[::-1])
    C=np.maximum(C,0)
    C=_waterfill(C, cur_t)
    F=fft(C,N)
    # phase continuity
    phase=np.angle(H)
    # F may be zero; where abs<1e-14, set H=0? But H[0] should not.
    absF=np.abs(F)
    # delta = arg(F) - 2*phase, wrap
    delta=(np.angle(F) - 2*phase + np.pi) % (2*np.pi) - np.pi
    newphase = phase + 0.5*delta
    Hnew = np.sqrt(absF) * np.exp(1j*newphase)
    # Ensure H[0] positive real: Hnew[0]=absF[0] **0.5 (since F0=1)
    Hnew[0]=math.sqrt(max(0,F[0].real)) + 0j
    # If N even, Nyquist should be real? Force Hnew[N//2] = math.sqrt(abs(F[N//2])) * (1 if F real part >=0 else 1j?) The newphase formula gives ±π/2; okay.
    anew = ifft(Hnew,N).real[:n]
    anew = np.maximum(anew, 0.0)
    s=anew.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0: fallback
    anew = anew/s
    # add tiny floor? optional: anew = (anew*(1-e) + eps)
    H=fft(zero pad anew)
    sc=fft_score(anew); if sc<best: update
    adapt cur_t:
       if sc < best_old? we already. Track improvement over last K.
       If improving: cur_t = max(min_t, cur_t*0.985)
       elif iter%10==0: cur_t = min(cur_t*1.05, max_cur)
  return best, score

Water fill 'C' must sum exactly 1. Implement:
def _waterfill(C,T):
  C=np.asarray(C,float).copy()
  C[~finite]=0; C=np.maximum(C,0)
  s=C.sum()
  if s<=0: return np.full(C.shape,1/C.size)
  C=C/s
  T=max(T, 1.0000001/C.size)
  for _ in range(10):
    cap=np.minimum(C,T)
    cs=cap.sum()
    if cs >= 1:
       return cap/cs
    res=1-cs
    room=T-cap
    m=room>1e-15
    if not m.any():
       return cap/cs # infeasible but T too low
    add=res*room[m]/room[m].sum()
    cap[m]+=add
  # after loop
  cap=np.minimum(C,T)
  cs=cap.sum(); if cs>0: cap/=cs
  return cap

Note waterfill with T close to 1/L gives flat. If T target maybe lower than current but not infeasible.

def _lbfgs_from(a, n, stop_time, beta, seed):
    z0 = np.log(np.clip(a,1e-10,1e6)); z0 = np.concatenate((z0[:-1], [0.0]))

       a_s = _softmax(z)
       b = _conv_full(a_s)
       U = _lse(b, beta)
       p = _softmax_beta(b, beta)
       h = _hankel_apply(p, a_s, n)

       grad = gz[:-1]
       # Replace nonfinite

def _hankel_apply(p,a,n):

      # top threshold loop

def _soft_p(b, beta):
   m=b.max()
   x=beta*(b-m)
   x=np.clip(x,-700,700)
   ex=np.exp(x)
   s=ex.sum()
   if s<=0: p=zeros; p[argmax(b)]=1; return 0? p
   p=ex/s
   return p
```

Let's implement `_stochastic_polish(a, stop_time, seed)`:
```
best=a; best_sc=score; rng=default_rng(seed); sigma=0.15; stuck=0
while time<stop:
  cand=a.copy()
  r=rng.random()
  if r<0.3:
    # log noise all
    z=np.log(np.clip(cand,1e-8,1e8)); z += rng.normal(0,sigma); cand=softmax(z)
  elif r<0.55:
    # block scale
    w=int(rng.integers(1, max(2,n//20))); sa=int(rng.integers(0,n-w)); cand[sa:sa+w]*=math.exp(rng.normal(0,0.2))
    normalize
  elif r<0.7:
    # pair active gradients? maybe not
    cand = 0.5*(a+a[::-1])
  elif r<0.85:
    # blend parent/uniform
    cand = 0.8*a + 0.2*(1/n)
  else:
    # random Fourier few iters? call _fourier_short?
    cand = _fourier_step(cand, few=3)
  sc=score; if sc<best_sc improve else adjust sigma
```

Let's design starts more concretely:

```
def _make_starts(n, parent, seed, want_count=4):
  rng=default_rng(seed)
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  starts=[]
  def add(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s>1e-12: a=a/s
    if a.shape!=(n,) or not finite: return
    # canonical: if len starts and allclose? skip? Not necessary.
    starts.append(a)
  # parent
  if parent is not None and parent.size>0:
     P=parent.size
     if n<=P:
        edges=np.floor(np.linspace(0,P,n+1)).astype(int)
        a=np.array([parent[s:e].mean() if e>s else parent[-1] for ...])
     else:
        idx=np.linspace(0,P-1,n).astype(int); a=parent[idx]
     a=np.maximum(a,1e-8) + rng.normal(0,0.05*max(1,a.mean()),n); add(a)
     # also exact parent if n==P (no noise)
     if n==parent.size: add(parent)
  # uniform
  add(np.ones(n))
  # endpoints gaussian
  for _ in range(2):
    w=rng.uniform(0.03,0.25)
    a=np.exp(-(x*x)/(2*w*w)) + np.exp(-((1-x)**2)/(2*w*w))
    a += 0.05
    add(a)
  # skewed endpoint
  for _ in range(2):
    a=0.2 + np.exp(-((x-rng.uniform(0,1))**2)/(2*0.15**2)) + 0.5*np.exp(-((x- (1-rng.uniform(0,1)))**2)/(2*0.1**2))
    add(a)
  # sine
  for _ in range(1):
    a=1 + 0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*rng.integers(2, max(3,n//8))*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)) + 0.3*np.cos(...)
    add(a)
  # random log
  a=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.7,n)); add(a)
  # monotone geometric
  r=rng.uniform(0.5,0.95); a=r**np.arange(n); also reverse
  return starts[:want_count]
```
Distinct enough. We can shuffle.

Maybe add "squared endpoints" from parent? ok.

Now, `_improve_one` schedule more precise:

```
def _improve_one(a, n, stop, seed, goal):
  start_time=time.monotonic()
  a=normalize(a)
  best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_norm(a)
  # Fourier
  if time < stop-10:
     t_four = min(stop-10, max(8, (stop-start_time)*0.25))
     cand, sc = _fourier_optimize(a,n, t_four, goal, seed)
     if sc<best_sc: best=cand; best_sc=sc
  # L-BFGS from best and maybe original
  remaining = stop-time
  if remaining > 30:
     t_lb = min(stop-30, max(30, remaining*0.45))
     # choose beta based on n and maybe start beta list
     betas = [2*n, 10*n]
     # if n small, 3
     sub_stop = time + t_lb
     # allocate half per beta
     for beta in betas:
       if time < sub_stop-10:
         cand, sc = _lbfgs_one(best, n, time+min(sub_stop, time+t_lb/(len(betas)-?)), beta, seed)
         update
  # LP
  if n <= 384 and time < stop-20:
     cand, sc = _lp_blend(best, n, min(stop-10, time + (stop-start_time)*0.10 + 5), seed)
  # coordinate + stochastic
  if time < stop-3:
     cand, sc = _local_polish(best, n, max(time+2, stop-2), seed)
  return best, best_sc
```
Need not over-nest; just use individual stop times.

def _lbfgs_one(a,n,stop,beta,seed):
   best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_norm(a)
   if time >= stop: return
   la=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-12,1e8)); la-=la.mean(); theta0=la[:-1].copy()
   theta0[~finite]=0
   state={'x':best,'sc':best_sc}
   def fg(theta):
      theta=np.asarray(theta,float)
      if not finite all: return inf, zeros
      z=np.empty(n); z[:-1]=np.clip(theta,-50,50); z[-1]= -np.sum(theta_clipped?)
```
Important: If we clip theta, z consistency with gradient? Use theta_c = np.clip(theta,-50,50), z[:-1]=theta_c, z[-1]=-theta_c.sum(). If theta outside, gradient maybe zero at bound; okay. Use theta_c in sum.
      a_s=_softmax_z(z)
      b=_conv_full(a_s)
      sc=2n*bmax
      if sc<state['sc']: state['x']=a_s.copy(); state['sc']=sc
      U,p=_lse_and_p(b,beta)
      if not finite U: return 1e100, zeros
      h=_hankel_apply(p,a_s,n)
      G=2*beta*a_s*(h - dot(a_s,h))
      # Replace nan
      G = np.where(np.isfinite(G), G, 0)
      grad = G[:-1] - G[-1]
      # If grad has nan, set 0
      return U, grad
   # initial maybe evaluate
   maxiter = 60 if n<=256 else 35
   try:
      res = minimize(theta0, fg, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-7, 'gtol':1e-3, 'maxcor':10, 'maxls':20, 'disp':False})
   except: pass
   # Final theta=res.x maybe evaluate
   x=state['x']; sc=state['sc']
   # exact call? caller will.
   return x, sc

```
def _fourier_optimize(a, n, stop, goal_score, seed):
  best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_norm(a)
  L=2*n-1; N=next_fast_len(L)
  a_pos=np.maximum(a,0); s=a_pos.sum(); if s<=0: return best,best_sc; a_pos/=s
  apad=np.zeros(N); apad[:n]=a_pos; H=fft(apad)
  cur_t = best_sc/(2*n)  # current max
  target_t = goal_score/(2*n) if goal_score>0 else cur_t*0.98
  min_t = max(1.0000001/L, target_t*0.98)
  max_t = max(cur_t*1.05, 1.01/L)
  # If cur_t <= target_t*1.001, still run with target lower 1.01? maybe.
  T = max(min_t, min(cur_t*0.995, cur_t))
  # We want decrease to min_t over iterations.
  last_improve=0
  no_improve=0
  for it in range(max_iter_e.g. 20000):
    if time>=stop or no_improve>...? break
    C=ifft(H*H,N).real
    C=C[:L]
    C=0.5*(C+C[::-1])
    C=np.maximum(C,0)
    C=_waterfill(C,T)
    F=fft(C,N)
    phase=np.angle(H)
    ang=np.angle(F)
    delta=(ang - 2*phase + math.pi) % (2*math.pi) - math.pi
    Hnew=np.sqrt(np.abs(F))*np.exp(1j*(phase+0.5*delta))
    Hnew[0]=math.sqrt(max(0.0,float(np.real(F[0])))) + 0j
    # maybe normalize Hnew magnitude? F0=1 so sum=1.
    x=ifft(Hnew,N).real
    # choose window offset with max positive sum? Need efficient.
    # We will take n consecutive with max sum of x after clipping? Use positive part sum.
    xp = np.maximum(x,0)
    # cyclic window sum of xp length n
    cum=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(xp), np.cumsum(xp)))? Simpler use convolution if N small:
       window_sums = np.convolve(np.concatenate([xp,xp]), np.ones(n), mode='valid')[:N]
    off = int(np.argmax(window_sums)) if window_sums.size>0 else 0
    a_new = xp[off:off+n] if off+n<=N else concatenate(xp[off:N], xp[: (off+n)%N])
    s_new=a_new.sum()
    if s_new <= 0.1*1.0? Actually total mass x sum? We want sum ~? H0=1, total positive may >1. Use if s_new <= 1e-6 fallback.
    # Maybe normalize to sum 1.
    a_new = a_new / s_new
    # optional add tiny floor to avoid zeros
    a_new = a_new + 1e-8
    a_new /= a_new.sum()
    sc=_score_norm(a_new)
    if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
       best=a_new.copy(); best_sc=sc; last_improve=it
       T = max(min_t, T*0.985)
    else:
       no_improve=it-last_improve
       # adapt T
       if it % 20 == 0:
          if no_improve > 50:
             T=min(max_t, T*1.05)
          else:
             T=max(min_t, T*0.995)
    # Update H from a_new
    apad2=np.zeros(N); apad2[:n]=a_new; H=fft(apad2)
  exact? caller.
  return best,best_sc
```

def _local_polish(a, n, stop, seed):

  # coordinate cycles
  idx_order=np.arange(n)

     if cycles>0 and cycles% n? check
     np.random.shuffle(idx_order)? Use rng.shuffle on copy.

     cycles+=1

        # random moves
        ...
        if cycles big break? maybe continue random until stop.
  return best,best_sc

The new zero-padded sequence's circular C is not necessarily the projected C; it's ifft(H_new^2), which we approximated? H_new^2=F=FFT(projected C exactly) before IFFT. The x=ifft(H_new) then zero-pad (take window) changes H, so C changes. But max may be okay. The window selection changes the resulting C from projected; not guaranteed invariance. But it's a heuristic. Choosing best positive window likely good.

Could avoid rotation to respect original support: take a_new=clip(x[:n],0). This may be stable. But if phase causes support shifted, we lose. Which is better? We can choose window only if the positive mass concentrated outside; but rotation may yield a different sequence score. Since we evaluate, if improved accept; if not keep best. It may also break monotonic but heuristic. Fine.

def _coord_search(a, i, stop, eps_window=1e-4):
  x=float(a[i]); x=min(max(x,0),1)
  def eval_y(y):
    s=1.0 - x + y
    if s <= 1e-12: return inf, None
    c=a.copy(); c[i]=y; c/=s
    sc=_score_norm(c)
    return sc,c
  samples=[x,0.0,1.0,0.5*x,2.0*x]
  samples += [x + nrng? deterministic] no rng
  vals=[]
  best_f=inf; best_c=None; best_y=x
  for y in samples:
    if 0<=y<=1:
       f,c=eval_y(y); update
  if best_c is None: ...
  # bracket
  if 0<best_y<1:
     w=max(0.05, 0.25*max(best_y,1-best_y))
     lo=max(0,best_y-w); hi=min(1,best_y+w)
  else:
     lo=0; hi=1
  # golden 10
  gr=(math.sqrt(5)+1)/2
  c=hi-(hi-lo)/gr; d=lo+(hi-lo)/gr
  fc,cc=eval_y(c); fd,dc=eval_y(d)
  for _ in range(12):
     if time>stop: break
     if fc < fd:
        hi=d; d=c; fd=fc; c=hi-(hi-lo)/gr; fc,cc=eval_y(c)
     else:
        lo=c; c=d; fc=fd; d=lo+(hi-lo)/gr; fd,dc=eval_y(d)
  xg=(lo+hi)/2; fg,cg=eval_y(xg)
  # choose min among best_c, cc,dc,cg (score finite)
  # But c arrays many; just choose best_y by scores and eval once if new.
```

Now, LP blend detailed code:
```
def _lp_blend(a, n, stop, seed):
  best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_norm(a)
  if n>512: return
  calls=0
  while time<stop-1 and calls<15:
    a_pos=np.maximum(best,1e-8).astype(float)
    s=a_pos.sum(); if s<=0: break; a_pos/=s
    b=_conv_full(a_pos); M=float(b.max())*1.0005 + 1e-12
    L=2*n-1
    A=np.zeros((L,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j,val in enumerate(a_pos):
       if val>0:
          A[j:j+n,j]=val
    c=-np.ones(n)
    try:
       res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(L,M), bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'disp':False})
    except Exception:
       try: res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(L,M), bounds=(0,None), method='interior-point')
       except: break
    if not res.success: break
    g=np.asarray(res.x,float)
    g=np.maximum(g,0)
    sg=g.sum()
    if not finite or sg<=0: break
    g/=sg
    improved=False
    for alpha in (0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0):
       cand=(1-alpha)*a_pos + alpha*g
       cand=np.maximum(cand,0)
       scand=cand.sum()
       if scand<=0: continue
       cand/=scand
       sc=_score_norm(cand)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
          best=cand; best_sc=sc; improved=True
    if not improved:
       # try extrapolate? or break after few no improve
       if calls>3: break
    calls+=1
  return best,best_sc
```

- `_fft_convolve` and `_conv_full`:
```
def _conv_full(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if a.size<=1: return np.array([a[0]*a[0]])
   n=a.size
   if n <= 320:
      c=np.convolve(a,a,'full')
   else:
      m=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
      F=rfft(a,m); c=irfft(F*F,m)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.asarray(c,float)
   c[~np.isfinite(c)]=0; c[c<0]=0
   return c
```
For n=1, convolve [a^2] fine.

- `_score_fast(a)`:
```

s=a.sum(); n=a.size
if s<0.01: return inf
b=_conv_full(a/s)
m=b.max() if b.size else 0
if not finite: return inf
return float(2*n*m)
```
Since a/s sum1. If a already sum1, okay. If a has zeros all, s=0 inf. For normalized, score =2n*max. Good.

if s<=0: p=zeros; idx=argmax(b); p[idx]=1; U=beta*m
else: U=beta*m+math.log(s); p=e/s
# zero tiny
return U,p
```
If beta huge and b-m negative large, exp underflows 0; if all underflow except m, s=1; U approx beta*m. Good.

- `_hankel_apply` as above.
```
def _hankel_apply(p,a,n):
   h=np.zeros(n)
   if n <= 320:
      idx=np.arange(n)
      H=p[idx[:,None]+idx[None,:]]
      h=H.dot(a)
      h=np.where(np.isfinite(h),h,0)
      return h
   pmax=p.max()
   thr=max(1e-14, pmax*1e-7)
   idx=np.where(p>=thr)[0]
   if idx.size==0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(p))])
   if idx.size>512:
      idx=np.argpartition(p, -512)[-512:]
   pw=p[idx]
   for k,w in zip(idx,pw):
       k=int(k); w=float(w)
       if w<=0: continue
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       if hi<lo: continue
       jlo=k-hi; jhi=k-lo
       h[lo:hi+1] += w*a[jlo:jhi+1][::-1]
   return h
```
Need p length at least n+n-1. If p length <2n-1? no.

If n=384 >320 top loop; p may be dense beta low. top 512 maybe okay.

- `_lbfgs_one`:
```
def _lbfgs_one(a, n, stop, beta, seed):
   a=_normalize(a)
   best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_fast(a)
   if time.monotonic() >= stop: return best,best_sc
   la=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-10,1e8)); la=la-la.mean(); theta0=la[:-1].copy()
   theta0[~finite]=0
   state={'x':best,'sc':best_sc}
   def fg(theta):
      theta=np.asarray(theta,float).ravel()
      if theta.shape[0] != n-1: theta = theta[:n-1] if ... else zeros
      if not np.isfinite(theta).all(): return 1e100, np.zeros(n-1)
      tc=np.clip(theta,-50,50)
      z=np.empty(n); z[:-1]=tc; z[-1]=-tc.sum()
      a_s=_softmax_z(z)
      b=_conv_full(a_s)
      m=float(b.max()) if b.size else 0
      sc=float(2*n*m)
      if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < state['sc']:
           state['x']=a_s.copy(); state['sc']=sc
      U,p=_lse_and_p(b,beta)
      if not np.isfinite(U): return 1e100, np.zeros(n-1)
      h=_hankel_apply(p,a_s,n)
      S=float(np.dot(a_s,h))
      G=2.0*beta*a_s*(h-S)
      G=np.where(np.isfinite(G),G,0.0)
      grad=G[:-1]-G[-1]
      grad=np.where(np.isfinite(grad),grad,0.0)
      # optional scale gradient if too large? L-BFGS handles but if >1e6 maybe clip
      gm=np.max(np.abs(grad))
      if np.isfinite(gm) and gm>1e6: grad*=1e6/gm
      return float(U), grad
   maxiter = 70 if n<=256 else 40
   try:
      res = minimize(theta0, fg, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-7,'gtol':1e-3,'maxcor':10,'maxls':20,'disp':False})
   except Exception:
      pass
   # Evaluate final theta if res exists
   try:
      th=res.x
      if th is not None:
        tc=np.clip(np.asarray(th,float).ravel()[:n-1],-50,50)
        ... a_s, sc if better update state
   except pass
   return state['x'], state['sc']
```
Need if n==1? skip. n>=2.

Potential issue: `scipy.optimize.minimize` with `jac=True` and `fg` returning tuple may fail if U is ndarray? return float. Good.

If `minimize` not imported? it is.

- `_fourier_optimize`: Need `_waterfill` before.
```
def _waterfill(C,T):
  C=np.asarray(C,float).ravel().copy()
  C[~finite]=0; C=np.maximum(C,0)
  s=C.sum()
  if s<=0: return np.full(C.size,1/C.size)
  C=C/s
  if not finite T or T<=0: T=1.1/C.size
  T=max(T, 1.000001/C.size)
  for _ in range(8):
    cap=np.minimum(C,T)
    cs=cap.sum()
    if cs >= 1 - 1e-12:
       return cap/cs
    res=1-cs
    room=T-cap
    pos=room>1e-14
    if not pos.any():
       return cap/max(cs,1e-300)
    add=res*room[pos]/room[pos].sum()
    cap[pos]+=add
  cap=np.minimum(C,T)
  cs=cap.sum()
  if cs>0: cap/=cs
  return cap
```
If after adding still exceeds T due roundoff, loop fixes maybe. Good.

Fourier:
```
def _fourier_optimize(a,n,stop,goal,seed):
  a=_normalize(a); best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_fast(a)
  if time>=stop: return
  L=2*n-1

    C[:L]=... wait C length N. We need make C length L:
    C=C[:L]
    C=0.5*(C + C[::-1])
    C=np.maximum(C,0)
    C=_waterfill(C,T)
    F=fft(C,N)
    phase=np.angle(H)
    ang=np.angle(F)
    delta=(ang - 2.0*phase + math.pi) % (2.0*math.pi) - math.pi
    Hnew=np.sqrt(np.abs(F))*np.exp(1j*(phase + 0.5*delta))
    # Clean nonfinite

    Hnew[0]=math.sqrt(max(0.0, float(np.real(F[0])))) + 0j

    cand_list=[]

       dn=np.maximum(d,0); s=dn.sum(); cand_list.append(dn/s)

       prefix=np.concatenate(([0.0],np.cumsum(xp2)))

          s=win.sum(); if s>1e-12: cand_list.append(win/s)
    for cand in cand_list:
       # maybe blend with old? no, normalize and tiny floor
       cand=np.maximum(cand,0)
       s=cand.sum()

       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best=cand.copy(); best_sc=sc
    # Update H from best (or even if not improved? use candidate with best? Use best for stability)

In `_local_polish`, after coordinate cycle no improvement, random moves. Need update best if improve. Use `sigma` variable. Pseudo:
```
def _local_polish(a,n,stop,seed):
  rng=default_rng(seed)
  best=_normalize(a); best_sc=_score_fast(best)
  cycles=0; sigma=0.06
  while time<stop-0.5:
    order=np.arange(n); rng.shuffle(order)
    improved_coord=0
    for i in order:
       if time>stop-0.5: break
       cand,sc=_coordinate_search(best,i,stop)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
          best=cand; best_sc=sc; improved_coord+=1
    cycles +=1
    if improved_coord == 0:
      # random perturbations
      r=rng.random()
      if r<0.4:
        z=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-12)); z += sigma*rng.normal(size=n); cand=_softmax_z(z)
      elif r<0.7:
        cand=best.copy(); w=int(rng.integers(1,max(2,n//20))); s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w)); cand[s:s+w]*=math.exp(rng.normal(0,0.25)); cand=_normalize(cand)
      elif r<0.85:
        cand=0.5*(best+best[::-1]); cand=_normalize(cand)
      else:
        cand=0.8*best + 0.2/n; cand=_normalize(cand)
      sc=_score_fast(cand)
      if sc<best_sc-1e-10:
        best=cand; best_sc=sc; sigma=max(0.03,0.8*sigma)
      else:
        sigma=min(0.8, sigma*1.15)
      if cycles > 6:
        # maybe break if stuck long
        if best_sc < 1.51? continue; if cycles > 15 and sigma>0.7: break
    else:
      sigma=max(0.03, sigma*0.9)
    if best_sc < 1.48: break # enough?
  return best,best_sc
```
Coordinate search itself may take time; stop inside golden. If n large, one cycle may take long; check before coordinate. Use if time > stop-2 break.

Coordinate search random? It uses deterministic samples. Good.

But if coordinate no improvement for many cycles, random sigma may jump; good.

Let's write _improve_one carefully with time allocations:
```
def _improve_one(a,n,stop,seed,goal):
  start=time.monotonic()
  a=_normalize(a)
  best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_fast(a)
  # Fourier
  if time.monotonic() < stop - 15:
    t_four = min(stop-15, max(8.0, (stop-start)*0.25))
    cand,sc=_fourier_optimize(a,n,t_four,goal,seed)
    if sc<best_sc: best,best_sc=cand,sc
  # LBFGS
  rem=stop-time.monotonic()
  if rem>40:
    # Try two betas
    betas=[int(max(50,3*n)), int(max(80,12*n))]
    substops=[]
    t_len=None
    total=rem*0.45
    t_alpha=total/(len(betas))
    for beta in betas:
      if time.monotonic() < stop-25:
         t_end=time.monotonic()+t_alpha
         cand,sc=_lbfgs_one(best,n,t_end,beta,seed+beta)
         if sc<best_sc: best,best_sc=cand,sc
  # LP
  if n <= 384 and time.monotonic() < stop-20:
    t_lp = min(stop-20, time.monotonic()+max(8.0,(stop-start)*0.15))
    cand,sc=_lp_blend(best,n,t_lp,seed)
    if sc<best_sc: best,best_sc=cand,sc
  # local polish
  if time.monotonic() < stop-3:
    cand,sc=_local_polish(best,n, max(time.monotonic()+1, stop-2), seed)
    if sc<best_sc: best,best_sc=cand,sc
  return best,best_sc
```
This may not use all time if Fourier 25%, LBFGS 45% of remaining, LP 15%, local rest? After LBFGS, rem maybe 55% of original; LP 15 original, local remaining ~? Good.

But if Fourier quick, LBFGS t_alpha based on rem*0.45; local gets rest. Fine.

We can also run LBFGS from original a if Fourier changed best? best after Fourier likely.

- `_generate_starts(n, parent, seed)` returns list. We'll implement with variants. Need avoid duplicates and too many. Use parent if not None. Use math.

Pseudo:
```
def _normalize(a): a=np.asarray(float).ravel(); a[~finite]=0; a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); return a/s if s>0 else ones
def _generate_starts(n,parent,seed):
  rng=default_rng(seed)
  starts=[]
  def add(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if a.size!=n: return
    a[~finite]=0; a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return
    a=a/s
    # skip if too similar to existing? check max abs diff <1e-6 maybe expensive but starts small.
    for old in starts:
       if np.max(np.abs(a-old))<1e-8: return
    starts.append(a)
  if parent is not None:
    p=np.asarray(parent,float).ravel(); p[~finite]=0; p=np.maximum(p,0); sp=p.sum(); if sp>0 and p.size>0:
       p=p/sp
       if n==p.size:
          add(p)
          add(p + 0.02*rng.normal(n))
       else:
          # resample by interpolation
          P=p.size
          idx=np.linspace(0,P-1,n)
          left=np.floor(idx).astype(int); right=np.minimum(left+1,P-1); frac=idx-left
          a=np.where(frac==? , p[left]*(1-frac)+p[right]*frac)
          add(np.maximum(a,1e-8)+0.03*max(1,n.mean())*np.abs(rng.normal(n)))
  add(np.ones(n))
  # U-shaped powers
  x=np.linspace(1e-4,1-1e-4,n)
  for g in (0.15,0.25,0.35,0.45):
    f=(x*(1-x))**(-g)
    add(f)
  # endpoint gaussians
  for _ in range(2):
    w=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.25))
    f=np.exp(-(x*x)/(2*w*w))+np.exp(-((1-x)**2)/(2*w*w))+0.05
    add(f)
  # skewed endpoints
  for _ in range(2):
    c=float(rng.uniform(0,1)); w1=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.3)); w2=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.3))
    f=np.exp(-((x-c)**2)/(2*w1*w1)) + 0.7*np.exp(-((x-1+c)**2)/(2*w2*w2))? w2 maybe near 0/1.
    # add small uniform
    add(f+0.05)
  # sine
  for _ in range(2):
    f1=1.0
    for _ in range(2):
       k=int(rng.integers(1, max(2,n//10)))
       phi=rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi); amp=rng.uniform(0.2,0.6)
       f1 += amp*np.sin(2*math.pi*k*x+phi)
    add(f1)
  # random lognormal
  f=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.6,n)); add(f)
  # geometric
  r=float(rng.uniform(0.5,0.9))
  f=r**np.arange(n); add(f); add(f[::-1])
  return starts
```
Need f negative for sine; add clip in add. Good.

def _get_parent():
  try: h=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
  except: h=None
  if h is None: return np.ones(32)
  try: p=np.asarray(h,float).ravel()
  except: p=np.ones(32)
  p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0); p=np.maximum(p,0)
  if p.size==0: p=np.ones(32)
  return p
```

`_normalize`: If a is scalar? a.ravel.
```
def _normalize(a):
    try:
        a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return np.ones(32,dtype=np.float64)/32
    if a.size==0:
        return np.ones(32,dtype=np.float64)/32
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)/a.size
    return a/s
```
If a is large but nonzero. Good.

`_score_fast`: If n==0 inf. Use clip 1000 maybe unnecessary but good.

```
def _score_fast(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return math.inf
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return math.inf
    b=_conv_full(a/s)
    if b.size==0: return math.inf
    m=float(np.max(b))
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m<0: return math.inf
    return float(2.0*a.size*m)
```
For normalized a sum1, score exact. If a/s sum1. Good.

`_conv_full`: For direct np.convolve can produce tiny negative due? input nonnegative so no. For FFT, clip.
```
def _conv_full(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return np.array([0.0])
   if n==1: return np.array([a[0]*a[0]])
   if n <= 320:
      c=np.convolve(a,a,'full')
   else:
      m=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
      F=rfft(a,m); c=irfft(F*F,m)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64)
   c[~np.isfinite(c)]=0.0
   c[c<0]=0.0
   return c
```
Good.

`_softmax_z`:
```
def _softmax_z(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if z.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
   z=np.where(np.isfinite(z),z,0.0)
   m=float(np.max(z))
   e=np.exp(z-m)
   s=float(np.sum(e))
   if not math.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
      return np.full(z.size,1.0/z.size,dtype=np.float64)
   return e/s
```
Good.

`_lse_and_p`: Ensure b finite.
```
def _lse_and_p(b,beta):
   b=np.asarray(b,dtype=np.float64)
   if b.size==0: return 0.0, np.array([1.0])
   b=np.where(np.isfinite(b),b,0.0)
   m=float(np.max(b))
   if not math.isfinite(m): m=0.0
   x=beta*(b-m)
   x=np.clip(x,-700,700)
   e=np.exp(x)
   s=float(e.sum())
   if s<=0 or not math.isfinite(s):
      p=np.zeros_like(b)
      idx=int(np.argmax(b)); p[idx]=1.0
      return beta*m, p
   U=beta*m + math.log(s)
   if not math.isfinite(U): U=beta*m
   p=e/s
   return float(U), p
```
Good.

`_hankel_apply`: Need p size may less than 2n-1 due? not. If p has length > 2n-1, outer index i+j max 2n-2, ok. Use p[:2n-1]? no.

```
def _hankel_apply(p,a,n):
   h=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
   if n<=0: return h
   if n<=320:
      idx=np.arange(n)
      H=p[idx[:,None]+idx[None,:]]
      h=np.dot(H,a)
      h=np.where(np.isfinite(h),h,0.0)
      return h
   pmax=float(np.max(p))
   if pmax<=0 or not finite: idx=np.argmax(p); w=1; ...
   thr=max(1e-14, pmax*1e-7)
   idx=np.where(p>=thr)[0]
   if idx.size==0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(p))])
   if idx.size>512:
      idx=np.argpartition(p,-512)[-512:]
   for k,w in zip(idx,p[idx]): ...
```
In top loop, if p length maybe >2n-1, k could >2n-2? But p from b length L. okay.
Segment: `orig = a[jlo:jhi+1][::-1]`; length = hi-lo+1. Check with k=0: lo=0, hi=0, jlo=0,jhi=0, h0 +=p0 a0. k=n-1: lo=0,hi=n-1,jlo=0,jhi=n-1, h[0:n]+=p_{n-1} a[n-1:0:-1] reversed: h_i = a[n-1-i]. T_{n-1} a_i = a[n-1-i]. Good. k=2n-2: lo=n-1,hi=n-1,jlo=n-1,jhi=n-1. Good.

Now `_waterfill`: If C all zeros, return uniform length C.size. C.size=L. Good.

```
def _waterfill(C,T):
    C=np.asarray(C,dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if C.size==0: return C
    C[~np.isfinite(C)]=0.0
    C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
    s=float(C.sum())
    if s<=0: return np.full(C.size,1.0/C.size)
    C=C/s
    if T is None or not math.isfinite(T) or T<=0: T=1.1/C.size
    T=max(float(T), 1.0/C.size * 1.000001)
    for _ in range(10):
       cap=np.minimum(C,T)
       cs=float(cap.sum())
       if cs >= 1.0 - 1e-12:
          if cs>0: return cap/cs
       res=1.0-cs
       room=T-cap
       pos=room>1e-14
       if not np.any(pos):
          return cap/max(cs,1e-300)
          # may exceed T, but T too low
       add=res*room[pos]/float(room[pos].sum())
       cap[pos]+=add
    cap=np.minimum(C,T)
    cs=float(cap.sum())
    if cs>0: cap=cap/cs
    return cap
```
If cs after normalization >1? `cap/cs` ensures sum1. Max maybe >T if cs>1? But cs<=1 because cap<=C sum1? Actually cap sum <1, so normalizing cap/cs can increase values above T. But only if cap.sum<1 and we can't fill due room sum small? The loop should fill. If T infeasible (<1/C.size), normalized would exceed T. We set T>=1/C.size, so feasible. Good.

def _fourier_optimize(a,n,stop,goal,seed):
   best=_normalize(a)
   best_sc=_score_fast(best)
   if not math.isfinite(best_sc): best_sc=1.0/n
   if time.monotonic() >= stop or n<2: return best,best_sc
   L=2*n-1
   N=next_fast_len(L)
   apad=np.zeros(N,dtype=np.float64); apad[:n]=best
   H=fft(apad)
   if not np.isfinite(H).all(): H=fft(apad)
   def cur_t():
      return max(1.0/L, (best_sc/(2*n) if math.isfinite(best_sc) else 1.0/L))
   tgt = goal/(2*n) if (goal is not None and math.isfinite(goal) and goal>0) else cur_t()
   min_t=max(1.000001/L, float(min(tgt, cur_t())*0.98 if tgt<cur_t() else cur_t()*0.98))? 
```

   min_t = max(1.000001/L, (goal/(2*n) if finite and goal>0 else cur_t()) * 0.98)
   max_t = max(cur_t() * 1.05, 1.01/L)
   T = max(min_t, min(cur_t() * 0.995, max_t))
```

Loop:
```
   last=0
   for it in range(20000):
      if time.monotonic() >= stop: break
      C=ifft(H*H,N).real
      C=C[:L]
      C=0.5*(C+C[::-1])
      C=np.maximum(C,0)
      C=_waterfill(C,T)
      F=fft(C,N)
      phase=np.angle(H)
      delta=(np.angle(F)-2*phase + math.pi) % (2*math.pi) - math.pi
      Hnew=np.sqrt(np.abs(F))*np.exp(1j*(phase + 0.5*delta))
      Hnew[~np.isfinite(Hnew)] = 0.0
      if F[0].real>0: Hnew[0]=math.sqrt(F[0].real)+0j
      x=ifft(Hnew,N).real
      x[~np.isfinite(x)]=0.0
      improved=False
      # direct
      d=np.maximum(x[:n],0)
      if d.sum()>1e-12:
         cand=d/d.sum(); sc=_score_fast(cand)
         if finite and sc<best_sc-1e-13:
            best=cand; best_sc=sc; improved=True
      # window
      xp=np.maximum(x,0)
      if xp.sum()>1e-12:
        xp2=np.concatenate((xp,xp))
        pref=np.concatenate(([0.0],np.cumsum(xp2)))
        sums=pref[n:n+N]-pref[:N]
        off=int(np.argmax(sums))
        if sums[off]>1e-12:
           ww = xp[off:off+n] if off+n<=N else np.concatenate((xp[off:N], xp[:off+n-N]))
           if ww.sum()>1e-12:
             cand=ww/ww.sum(); sc=_score_fast(cand)
             if finite and sc<best_sc-1e-13: ...
      # update H from best (could be best just improved)
      apad2=np.zeros(N); apad2[:n]=best; H=fft(apad2)
      if improved: last=it
      if it % 10 == 0:
         if it - last > 50:
            # raise T
            cur=cur_t()
            T=min(max_t, max(T*1.05, min_t*1.1))
         else:
            T=max(min_t, T*0.985)
         # also if T > cur*1.2 and no improve, reduce max? no
   return best,best_sc
```
Note if best not improved and T oscillates, H changes because waterfill uses new T but H from best unchanged. Good.

`_lbfgs_one`: Need if n<2 return.
```
def _lbfgs_one(a,n,stop,beta,seed):
   best=_normalize(a); best_sc=_score_fast(best)
   if n<2 or time>=stop: return best,best_sc
   la=np.log(np.clip(best,1e-10,1e8)); la-=la.mean(); theta0=la[:-1].copy(); theta0[~finite]=0
   state={'x':best,'sc':best_sc}
   def fg(theta):
      theta=np.asarray(theta,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
      if theta.size != n-1:
          theta = np.resize(theta, n-1)
      if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
          return 1e100, np.zeros(n-1)
      tc=np.clip(theta,-50,50)
      z=np.empty(n); z[:-1]=tc; z[-1]=-tc.sum()
      a_s=_softmax_z(z)
      b=_conv_full(a_s)
      m=float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0
      sc=float(2*n*m)
      if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < state['sc']:
          state['x']=a_s.copy(); state['sc']=sc
      U,p=_lse_and_p(b,float(beta))
      if not math.isfinite(U): return 1e100, np.zeros(n-1)
      h=_hankel_apply(p,a_s,n)
      S=float(np.dot(a_s,h))
      G=2.0*beta*a_s*(h-S)
      G=np.where(np.isfinite(G),G,0.0)
      grad=G[:-1]-G[-1]
      grad=np.where(np.isfinite(grad),grad,0.0)
      gm=float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) if grad.size else 0.0
      if math.isfinite(gm) and gm>1e6: grad*=1e6/gm
      return float(U), grad
   maxiter=70 if n<=256 else (45 if n<=512 else 30)
   try:
       res=minimize(theta0, fg, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={...})
       # final eval theta
       th=res.x
       tc=np.clip(np.asarray(th,float).ravel()[:n-1],-50,50)
       z=...; a_s; b; sc; if sc<state['sc'] update
   except Exception:
       pass
   return state['x'], state['sc']
```
Options dict: `{'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-7, 'gtol': 1e-3, 'maxcor': 10, 'maxls': 20, 'disp': False}`. `ftol` absolute in U; U can be beta*m + log, maybe large ~ beta*0.004=1.5, so 1e-7 okay. `gtol` 1e-3. Good.

If res.x has nan, final eval skip. If res is None? no.

Potential issue: L-BFGS line search may call fg with theta shape (n-1,1)? ravel.

`_coordinate_search`:
```
def _coordinate_search(a, i, stop):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   n=a.size; x=float(np.clip(a[i],0,1))
   def eval_y(y):
      y=float(y)
      if y<0 or y>1: return math.inf, None
      s=1.0 - x + y
      if s<=1e-12: return math.inf, None
      c=a.copy(); c[i]=y; c/=s
      sc=_score_fast(c)
      return sc,c
   samples=[x,0.0,1.0,0.5*x,2.0*x, x+0.01, x-0.01]
   best_f=math.inf; best_y=x; best_c=None
   for y in samples:
      if 0<=y<=1:
         f,c=eval_y(y)
         if f<best_f: best_f=f; best_y=y; best_c=c
   if best_c is None:
      return a.copy(), _score_fast(a)
   if 0<best_y<1:
      width=max(0.05, 0.25*max(best_y, 1-best_y))
      lo=max(0,best_y-width); hi=min(1,best_y+width)
   else:
      lo,hi=0.0,1.0
   gr=(1+math.sqrt(5))/2
   c=hi-(hi-lo)/gr; d=lo+(hi-lo)/gr
   fc,cc=eval_y(c); fd,dc=eval_y(d)
   for _ in range(12):
      if time.monotonic()>stop: break
      if fc < fd:
         hi=d; d=c; fd=fc; c=hi-(hi-lo)/gr; fc,cc=eval_y(c)
      else:
         lo=c; c=d; fc=fd; d=lo+(hi-lo)/gr; fd,dc=eval_y(d)
   xg=(lo+hi)/2; fg,cg=eval_y(xg)
   # choose best among best_c, cc, dc, cg
   options=[]
   for f,ccand in ((best_f,best_c),(fc,cc),(fd,dc),(fg,cg)):
       if ccand is not None and math.isfinite(f) and f<best_f:
           best_f=f; best_c=ccand
   if best_c is None or not math.isfinite(best_f): return a.copy(), _score_fast(a)
   return best_c, best_f
```
Potential bug: We update best_f inside options after loop; but if cc/dc less than best_f, best_c updated; if not, keep. Good. But `fc,cc` after loop may be same as fd? okay.

If initial samples include x and best_c current; if all invalid, return a.

Time: The golden section recomputes f(c) and f(d) again after bracket; okay.

`_local_polish`: Need `rng.shuffle(order)` where order list or array. NumPy random generator has shuffle for array. Use `order=np.arange(n); rng.shuffle(order)`. If n large, for each cycle full. For n>512, sample 48 coords:
```
if n>512:
   order=rng.choice(n, size=min(48,n), replace=False)
else: full
```
But if random, coordinate cycles might miss. For n>512, okay. We can also full for parent 192.

Use time check.
```
def _local_polish(a,n,stop,seed):
   best=_normalize(a); best_sc=_score_fast(best)
   if n<2 or time>=stop: return best,best_sc
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   cycles=0; sigma=0.05; no_imp_total=0
   while time.monotonic() < stop-0.5:
      if n>512:
         order=rng.choice(n, size=min(48,n), replace=False)
      else:
         order=np.arange(n); rng.shuffle(order)
      improved=0
      for i in order:
         if time.monotonic() > stop-0.5: break
         cand,sc=_coordinate_search(best,int(i),stop)
         if sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
             best=cand; best_sc=sc; improved+=1
      cycles+=1
      if improved>0:
          no_imp_total=0; sigma=max(0.02, sigma*0.85)
      else:
          no_imp_total+=1
          r=rng.random()
          if r<0.45:
             z=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-12)); z += sigma*rng.normal(size=n); cand=_softmax_z(z)
          elif r<0.70:
             cand=best.copy()
             w=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//20)))
             w=min(w, n)
             s=int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n-w+1)))
             cand[s:s+w] *= math.exp(float(rng.normal(0.0,0.25)))
             cand=_normalize(cand)
          elif r<0.85:
             cand=0.5*(best + best[::-1]); cand=_normalize(cand)
          else:
             cand=0.8*best + 0.2*float(1.0/n); cand=_normalize(cand)
          sc=_score_fast(cand)
          if sc<best_sc-1e-10:
             best=cand; best_sc=sc; no_imp_total=0; sigma=max(0.02, sigma*0.85)
          else:
             sigma=min(0.9, sigma*1.15)
          if no_imp_total > 25 and best_sc < 100:
             # give up to save time? Maybe if many cycles no progress
             if best_sc < 1.52 or cycles > 60: break
      if best_sc < 1.45: break
   return best,best_sc
```
If coordinate cycles keep improving, continue until stop. If no_imp_total >25 and best near target, break. Fine.

Potential issue: `_coordinate_search` uses current a inside closure; when order loops, best updates; fine.

`_lp_blend`: Need n<=600? Set <=512. Build A dense. For n=512 A 1023x512 ~ 8MB. Good.
```
def _lp_blend(a,n,stop,seed):
   best=_normalize(a); best_sc=_score_fast(best)
   if n<2 or n>512 or time>=stop: return best,best_sc
   calls=0; no=0
   while time.monotonic()+0.5 < stop and calls<12:
      a_pos=np.maximum(best,1e-8); s=a_pos.sum(); a_pos/=s
      b=_conv_full(a_pos); M=float(b.max())*1.0005 + 1e-12
      L=2*n-1
      A=np.zeros((L,n), dtype=np.float64)
      for j,val in enumerate(a_pos):
         if val>0:
            A[j:j+n,j]=float(val)
      c=-np.ones(n)
      b_ub=np.full(L,M)
      try:
         res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0.0,None), method='highs', options={'disp':False})
      except Exception:
         try:
            res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0.0,None), method='interior-point')
         except Exception:
            break
      if res is None or not getattr(res,'success',False): break
      g=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
      g=np.where(np.isfinite(g),g,0.0); g=np.maximum(g,0)
      sg=float(g.sum())
      if sg<=0: break
      g/=sg
      improved=False
      for alpha in (0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0):
         cand=(1-alpha)*a_pos + alpha*g
         cand=np.maximum(cand,0); scs=cand.sum()
         if scs<=0: continue
         cand/=scs
         sc=_score_fast(cand)
         if math.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_sc-1e-10:
             best=cand; best_sc=sc; improved=True
      calls+=1
      if not improved:
         no+=1
         if no>=4: break
      else: no=0
   return best,best_sc
```

```
def _generate_starts(n,parent,seed):
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  starts=[]
  def add(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size != n: return
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return
    a=a/s
    for old in starts:
       if old.size==n and np.max(np.abs(a-old)) < 1e-7:
          return
    starts.append(a)
  if parent is not None:
    p=_normalize(parent) if np.size(parent)>0 else None
    if p is not None and p.size>0:
       P=p.size
       if n==P:
          add(p)
          add(p + 0.05*max(1.0,float(p.mean()))*np.abs(rng.normal(size=n)))
       else:
          idx=np.linspace(0,P-1,n)
          left=np.floor(idx).astype(int); left=np.clip(left,0,P-1)
          right=np.minimum(left+1,P-1)
          frac=idx-left
          a=p[left]*(1-frac)+p[right]*frac
          a=np.maximum(a,1e-6)
          a += 0.03*max(1.0,float(a.mean()))*np.abs(rng.normal(size=n))
          add(a)
  add(np.ones(n))
  x=np.linspace(1e-4, 1-1e-4, n)
  for g in (0.12,0.22,0.32,0.45):
     f=(x*(1-x))**(-g)
     add(f)
  for _ in range(2):
     w=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.25))
     f=np.exp(-(x*x)/(2*w*w)) + np.exp(-((1-x)**2)/(2*w*w)) + 0.05
     add(f)
  for _ in range(2):
     c1=float(rng.uniform(0.0,1.0)); c2=1.0-c1 if c1<0.5 else 0.5-c1? hmm.
     w1=float(rng.uniform(0.04,0.25)); w2=float(rng.uniform(0.04,0.25))
     f=np.exp(-((x-c1)**2)/(2*w1*w1)) + 0.8*np.exp(-((x-c2)**2)/(2*w2*w2)) + 0.05
     add(f)
  for _ in range(2):
     f=np.ones(n)
     for _ in range(2):
        k=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//8)))
        phi=float(rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi)); amp=float(rng.uniform(0.15,0.55))
        f += amp*np.sin(2*math.pi*k*x + phi)
     add(f)
  f=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.6,n)); add(f)
  r=float(rng.uniform(0.55,0.9))
  f=r**np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64); add(f); add(f[::-1])
  # maybe zero? no
  if not starts: starts=[np.ones(n)/n]
  return starts
```

c2 = rng.choice([0.0,1.0,1.0-c1])

`_optimize_length`:
```
def _optimize_length(n, stop, seed, parent, goal):
  if n<2: return None, math.inf
  if time.monotonic() > stop: return None, inf
  starts=_generate_starts(n,parent,seed)
  scored=[]
  for a in starts:
     sc=_score_fast(a)
     if math.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,a))
  scored.sort(key=lambda z:z[0])
  # Select up to 4, or less if time. Limit candidate starts if time insufficient.
  max_starts = 4 if n<=384 else 3
  max_starts = min(max_starts, len(scored), max(1, int((stop-time.monotonic())//25)))
  starts=[a for sc,a in scored[:max_starts]]
  best=None; best_sc=math.inf
  idx=0
  for a in starts:
     if time.monotonic() > stop - 10: break
     # allocate remaining fairly
     rem=stop-time.monotonic()
     left=len(starts)-idx
     alloc=rem/max(1,left)
     if idx==0: alloc=min(rem, alloc*1.5)
     s_stop=min(stop-5, time.monotonic()+alloc)
     if s_stop <= time.monotonic()+5: break
     cand,sc=_improve_one(a,n,s_stop,seed+idx*131,goal)
     if cand is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_sc:
         best=cand; best_sc=sc
     idx+=1
  return best,best_sc
```
If time very low, max_starts 0; use one? Use max(1, int((stop-time)//25)) but if stop-time<25, int0. Maybe if budget for length 30s, int1 if //25. Use `max(1, min(4, int((stop-time.monotonic()) // 30)))`? If alloc 60, 2. Fine. Simpler: `max_starts = 4`; _improve_one checks time. But if many starts and time low, first may use most. We can cap.

```
def _improve_one(a,n,stop,seed,goal):
  best=_normalize(a); best_sc=_score_fast(best)
  start=time.monotonic()
  if time.monotonic() < stop - 10:
     t_four=min(stop-10, max(6.0, (stop-start)*0.25))
     cand,sc=_fourier_optimize(best,n,t_four,goal,seed)
     if cand is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_sc:
         best=cand; best_sc=sc
  if n <= 512 and time.monotonic() < stop - 30:
     rem=stop-time.monotonic()
     t_total=rem*0.45
     betas=[int(max(50, 2*n)), int(max(80, 10*n))]
     t_each=t_total/len(betas)
     for bi,beta in enumerate(betas):
        if time.monotonic() < stop - 25:
           t_end=min(stop-20, time.monotonic()+t_each)
           cand,sc=_lbfgs_one(best,n,t_end,beta,seed+beta)
           if cand is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_sc:
              best=cand; best_sc=sc
  if n <= 384 and time.monotonic() < stop - 15:
     t_lp=min(stop-12, time.monotonic()+max(6.0, (stop-start)*0.12))
     cand,sc=_lp_blend(best,n,t_lp,seed+999)
     if cand is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_sc:
        best=cand; best_sc=sc
  if time.monotonic() < stop - 3:
     t_end=stop-2
     cand,sc=_local_polish(best,n,t_end,seed+777)
     if cand is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_sc:
        best=cand; best_sc=sc
  return best,best_sc
```

In some SciPy versions, L-BFGS-B expects `fun` to return a tuple only if `jac=True`, but the tuple's first element must be scalar. Good. If it doesn't, exception caught. No fallback. Could we add a very simple gradient fallback if `minimize` raises? We can set a flag `used=False`; if exception, run manual `_manual_lse`? But time.

Maybe implement fallback `_manual_lse` small? Let's consider adding a lightweight function to improve robustness. It can use current best and beta for time. But code length. We already have `_local_polish` and Fourier. L-BFGS failure unlikely.

Potential issue with `res.x` after exception? set None.

Now, consider if `np.isfinite` on complex in Fourier: `Hnew[~np.isfinite(Hnew)] = 0.0` with complex works: `np.isfinite` returns bool array. Good.

Now, think about exact evaluator's sum_a <0.01 returns inf. Our normalized sum=1. If due underflow `best_seq` all zeros? _normalize prevents. Final finalize [1.0] fallback.

Now, let's consider if the target can be improved by returning a subsequence (downsampling) of parent? Length changes. We try other lengths.

Potential edge: If parent length not in `ordered` due cap 8 and parent missing? If parent missing Lp=0, ordered from extra sorted; okay. If parent present, ordered first parent then up to 7 others. Good.

Now, maybe need to include `height_sequence_1` directly in evaluate initial: `evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_seq])`, where best_seq=parent (ndarray). It's okay. We shouldn't copy parent values into response; we don't.

Now, write final code.

Double-check `_hankel_apply` for top loop when `p` length less than `k`? p is length 2n-1; k from p indices. `jlo=k-hi`, `jhi=k-lo` maybe negative? For k>=0. If k<0 no. If k >2n-2 no. lo/hi ensure j range within [0,n-1]. Because hi=min(n-1,k), lo=max(0,k-n+1). Then k-hi <= n-1? If hi=k, jlo=0; if hi=n-1, jlo=k-n+1=lo? Actually jlo = k-hi = k-n+1, same as lo? Wait lo index in a for i. For k>n-1, hi=n-1, jlo=k-(n-1) which is ≥0; jhi=k-lo=k-(k-n+1)=n-1. Range jlo:jhi+1 valid. Good.

Now, `_coordinate_search` bracket if best_y 0 or 1 uses full [0,1]; golden on full may evaluate near current samples. Good.

`_local_polish` if coordinate search returns same best_sc, no improvement. Random moves. If coordinate search for all coords is expensive and no improvement but n=192, each cycle maybe 2s; 25 cycles 50s. okay.

Now, possible improvement: In `_local_polish`, coordinate search order full for n=192; if each coordinate takes ~0.001s? maybe 0.2s/cycle; 25 cycles 5s. Good.

Now, exact consider in loop: It may call evaluate_sequence for candidate with fast_score much worse? Condition fast > best +0.05 skip. If fast is inf skip. Good. But for a candidate with fast 1.52 and best inf, exact called. Fine.

Potential bug: `best_score` initial from exact maybe 1.513. If a candidate fast 1.52 due exact 1.514 slightly worse, condition fast < best+0.05 true, exact called, no update. Fine.

Now, if `best_score` is inf, condition fast < inf true, exact called. Good.

Now, let's include comments? minimal.

Let's write final code now. Ensure no `nonlocal` at module level. All good.

One more detail: `evaluate_sequence` and `height_sequence_1` pre-imported; our code references `evaluate_sequence` in propose. If not defined (in local test), NameError? The task says pre-imported. Good.

Now, final output.

```python
import math
import time
import warnings

import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len, fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog

warnings.filterwarnings("ignore")

_TARGET_BOUND = 1.50274349


def _get_parent():
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    except Exception:
        h = None

    if h is None:
        return np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0

    try:
        p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        p = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0

    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)

    if p.size == 0 or p.sum() <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0

    return p


def _normalize(a):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0

    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())

    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)

    return a / s


def _conv_full(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size

    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0] * a[0]], dtype=np.float64)

    if n <= 320:
        c = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    else:
        m = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
        F = rfft(a, m)
        c = irfft(F * F, m)[: (2 * n - 1)]

    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0.0
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def _score_fast(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return math.inf

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())

    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return math.inf

    b = _conv_full(a / s)
    if b.size == 0:
        return math.inf

    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        return math.inf

    return float(2.0 * a.size * m)


def _softmax_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z - m)
    s = float(np.sum(e))

    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / float(z.size), dtype=np.float64)

    return e / s


def _lse_and_p(b, beta):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if b.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        m = 0.0

    x = beta * (b - m)
    x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(np.sum(e))

    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros_like(b, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = int(np.argmax(b))
        p[idx] = 1.0
        return float(beta * m), p

    U = beta * m + math.log(s)
    if not math.isfinite(U):
        U = beta * m

    p = e / s
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    return float(U), p


def _hankel_apply(p, a, n):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return h

    if n <= 320:
        idx = np.arange(n)
        H = p[idx[:, None] + idx[None, :]]
        h = np.dot(H, a)
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        return h

    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    pmax = float(np.max(p)) if p.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(pmax) or pmax <= 0.0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(p))])
        w = 1.0
        k = int(idx[0]) if idx.size else 0
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if hi >= lo:
            jlo = k - hi
            jhi = k - lo
            h[lo:hi + 1] = w * a[jlo:jhi + 1][::-1]
        return h

    thr = max(1e-14, pmax * 1e-7)
    idx = np.where(p >= thr)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(p))])
    if idx.size > 512:
        idx = np.argpartition(p, -512)[-512:]

    pw = p[idx]
    for k, w in zip(idx, pw):
        k = int(k)
        w = float(w)
        if not math.isfinite(w) or w <= 0.0:
            continue

        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if hi < lo:
            continue

        jlo = k - hi
        jhi = k - lo
        h[lo:hi + 1] += w * a[jlo:jhi + 1][::-1]

    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    return h


def _waterfill(C, T):
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if C.size == 0:
        return C

    C[~np.isfinite(C)] = 0.0
    C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
    s = float(C.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(C.size, 1.0 / C.size, dtype=np.float64)

    C = C / s
    if T is None or not math.isfinite(T) or T <= 0.0:
        T = 1.1 / C.size

    T = max(float(T), (1.0 / C.size) * 1.000001)

    for _ in range(10):
        cap = np.minimum(C, T)
        cs = float(cap.sum())
        if cs >= 1.0 - 1e-12:
            if cs > 0.0:
                return cap / cs

        res = 1.0 - cs
        room = T - cap
        pos = room > 1e-14
        if not np.any(pos):
            return cap / max(cs, 1e-300)

        room_sum = float(room[pos].sum())
        if room_sum > 0.0:
            cap[pos] += res * room[pos] / room_sum

    cap = np.minimum(C, T)
    cs = float(cap.sum())
    if cs > 0.0:
        cap = cap / cs
    return cap


def _fourier_optimize(a, n, stop, goal, seed):
    best = _normalize(a)
    best_sc = _score_fast(best)

    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= stop:
        return best, best_sc

    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_fast_len(L)

    apad = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    apad[:n] = best
    H = fft(apad)
    if not np.isfinite(H).all():
        H = fft(apad)

    def current_t():
        if math.isfinite(best_sc) and best_sc > 0.0:
            return max(1.0 / L, best_sc / (2.0 * n))
        return 1.0 / L

    cur_t = current_t()
    if goal is not None and math.isfinite(goal) and goal > 0.0:
        tgt_t = float(goal) / (2.0 * n)
    else:
        tgt_t = cur_t

    min_t = max(1.000001 / L, tgt_t * 0.98)
    max_t = max(cur_t * 1.05, 1.01 / L)
    T = max(min_t, min(cur_t * 0.995, max_t))

    last_improve = 0

    for it in range(20000):
        if time.monotonic() >= stop:
            break

        C = ifft(H * H, N).real
        C = C[:L]
        C = 0.5 * (C + C[::-1])
        C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
        C = _waterfill(C, T)

        F = fft(C, N)
        phase = np.angle(H)
        delta = (np.angle(F) - 2.0 * phase + math.pi) % (2.0 * math.pi) - math.pi
        Hnew = np.sqrt(np.abs(F)) * np.exp(1j * (phase + 0.5 * delta))
        Hnew[~np.isfinite(Hnew)] = 0.0

        if math.isfinite(float(np.real(F[0]))) and float(np.real(F[0])) > 0.0:
            Hnew[0] = math.sqrt(float(np.real(F[0]))) + 0.0j

        x = ifft(Hnew, N).real
        x[~np.isfinite(x)] = 0.0

        improved = False

        d = np.maximum(x[:n], 0.0)
        sd = float(d.sum())
        if sd > 1e-12:
            cand = d / sd
            sc = _score_fast(cand)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                best = cand.copy()
                best_sc = sc
                improved = True

        xp = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
        sxp = float(xp.sum())
        if sxp > 1e-12:
            xp2 = np.concatenate((xp, xp))
            pref = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(xp2)))
            sums = pref[n:n + N] - pref[:N]
            off = int(np.argmax(sums)) if sums.size else 0
            if off >= 0 and sums[off] > 1e-12:
                if off + n <= N:
                    win = xp[off:off + n]
                else:
                    win = np.concatenate((xp[off:N], xp[: off + n - N]))
                sw = float(win.sum())
                if sw > 1e-12:
                    cand = win / sw
                    sc = _score_fast(cand)
                    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                        best = cand.copy()
                        best_sc = sc
                        improved = True

        apad2 = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        apad2[:n] = best
        H = fft(apad2)

        if improved:
            last_improve = it

        if it % 10 == 0:
            if it - last_improve > 50:
                cur = current_t()
                T = min(max_t, max(T * 1.05, min_t * 1.1))
                T = min(T, max(T, cur * 1.15))
            else:
                T = max(min_t, T * 0.985)

    return best, best_sc


def _lbfgs_one(a, n, stop, beta, seed):
    best = _normalize(a)
    best_sc = _score_fast(best)

    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= stop:
        return best, best_sc

    beta = float(beta)
    la = np.log(np.clip(best, 1e-10, 1e8))
    la = la - float(np.mean(la))
    theta0 = la[:-1].copy()
    theta0[~np.isfinite(theta0)] = 0.0

    state = {"x": best.copy(), "sc": best_sc}

    def fg(theta):
        theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if theta.size != n - 1:
            theta = np.resize(theta, n - 1)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
            return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

        tc = np.clip(theta, -50.0, 50.0)
        z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        z[:-1] = tc
        z[-1] = -float(np.sum(tc))

        a_s = _softmax_z(z)
        b = _conv_full(a_s)
        m = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
        sc = float(2.0 * n * m)

        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < state["sc"]:
            state["x"] = a_s.copy()
            state["sc"] = sc

        U, p = _lse_and_p(b, beta)
        if not math.isfinite(U):
            return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

        h = _hankel_apply(p, a_s, n)
        S = float(np.dot(a_s, h))
        G = 2.0 * beta * a_s * (h - S)
        G = np.where(np.isfinite(G), G, 0.0)

        grad = G[:-1] - G[-1]
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)

        gm = float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) if grad.size else 0.0
        if math.isfinite(gm) and gm > 1e6:
            grad *= 1e6 / gm

        return float(U), grad

    maxiter = 70 if n <= 256 else (45 if n <= 512 else 30)
    res = None

    try:
        res = minimize(
            theta0,
            fg,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options={
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "ftol": 1e-7,
                "gtol": 1e-3,
                "maxcor": 10,
                "maxls": 20,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
    except Exception:
        res = None

    try:
        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            th = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if th.size != n - 1:
                th = np.resize(th, n - 1)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
                tc = np.clip(th, -50.0, 50.0)
                z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
                z[:-1] = tc
                z[-1] = -float(np.sum(tc))
                a_s = _softmax_z(z)
                b = _conv_full(a_s)
                sc = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(b))) if b.size else math.inf
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < state["sc"]:
                    state["x"] = a_s.copy()
                    state["sc"] = sc
    except Exception:
        pass

    return state["x"], state["sc"]


def _coordinate_search(a, i, stop):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    x = float(np.clip(a[i], 0.0, 1.0))

    def eval_y(y):
        y = float(y)
        if y < 0.0 or y > 1.0:
            return math.inf, None

        s = 1.0 - x + y
        if s <= 1e-12:
            return math.inf, None

        c = a.copy()
        c[i] = y
        c /= s
        sc = _score_fast(c)
        return sc, c

    samples = [x, 0.0, 1.0, 0.5 * x, 2.0 * x, x + 0.01, x - 0.01]
    best_f = math.inf
    best_y = x
    best_c = None

    for y in samples:
        if 0.0 <= y <= 1.0:
            f, c = eval_y(y)
            if math.isfinite(f) and f < best_f:
                best_f = f
                best_y = y
                best_c = c

    if best_c is None:
        return a.copy(), _score_fast(a)

    if 0.0 < best_y < 1.0:
        width = max(0.05, 0.25 * max(best_y, 1.0 - best_y))
        lo = max(0.0, best_y - width)
        hi = min(1.0, best_y + width)
    else:
        lo, hi = 0.0, 1.0

    gr = (1.0 + math.sqrt(5.0)) / 2.0
    c = hi - (hi - lo) / gr
    d = lo + (hi - lo) / gr
    fc, cc = eval_y(c)
    fd, dc = eval_y(d)

    for _ in range(12):
        if time.monotonic() > stop:
            break

        if fc < fd:
            hi = d
            d = c
            fd = fc
            c = hi - (hi - lo) / gr
            fc, cc = eval_y(c)
        else:
            lo = c
            c = d
            fc = fd
            d = lo + (hi - lo) / gr
            fd, dc = eval_y(d)

    xg = (lo + hi) / 2.0
    fg, cg = eval_y(xg)

    for f, cand in ((best_f, best_c), (fc, cc), (fd, dc), (fg, cg)):
        if cand is not None and math.isfinite(f) and f < best_f:
            best_f = f
            best_c = cand

    if best_c is None or not math.isfinite(best_f):
        return a.copy(), _score_fast(a)

    return best_c, best_f


def _local_polish(a, n, stop, seed):
    best = _normalize(a)
    best_sc = _score_fast(best)

    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= stop:
        return best, best_sc

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cycles = 0
    sigma = 0.05
    no_imp = 0

    while time.monotonic() < stop - 0.5:
        if n > 512:
            order = rng.choice(n, size=min(48, n), replace=False)
        else:
            order = np.arange(n)
            rng.shuffle(order)

        improved = 0
        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() > stop - 0.5:
                break

            cand, sc = _coordinate_search(best, int(i), stop)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
                best = cand
                best_sc = sc
                improved += 1

        cycles += 1

        if improved > 0:
            no_imp = 0
            sigma = max(0.02, sigma * 0.85)
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            r = float(rng.random())

            if r < 0.45:
                z = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-12))
                z += sigma * rng.normal(size=n)
                cand = _softmax_z(z)
            elif r < 0.70:
                cand = best.copy()
                w = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 20)))
                w = min(w, n)
                s = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
                cand[s:s + w] *= math.exp(float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25)))
                cand = _normalize(cand)
            elif r < 0.85:
                cand = 0.5 * (best + best[::-1])
                cand = _normalize(cand)
            else:
                cand = 0.8 * best + 0.2 * float(1.0 / n)
                cand = _normalize(cand)

            sc = _score_fast(cand)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
                best = cand
                best_sc = sc
                no_imp = 0
                sigma = max(0.02, sigma * 0.85)
            else:
                sigma = min(0.9, sigma * 1.15)

            if no_imp > 25 and (best_sc < 1.52 or cycles > 60):
                break

        if best_sc < 1.45:
            break

    return best, best_sc


def _lp_blend(a, n, stop, seed):
    best = _normalize(a)
    best_sc = _score_fast(best)

    if n < 2 or n > 512 or time.monotonic() >= stop:
        return best, best_sc

    calls = 0
    no_imp = 0

    while calls < 12 and time.monotonic() + 0.5 < stop:
        a_pos = np.maximum(best, 1e-8)
        s = float(a_pos.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            break
        a_pos = a_pos / s

        b = _conv_full(a_pos)
        M = float(np.max(b)) * 1.0005 + 1e-12
        if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        L = 2 * n - 1
        A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for j, val in enumerate(a_pos):
            if val > 0.0:
                A[j:j + n, j] = float(val)

        cobj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        b_ub = np.full(L, M, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

        res = None
        try:
            res = linprog(
                cobj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"disp": False},
            )
        except Exception:
            try:
                res = linprog(
                    cobj,
                    A_ub=A,
                    b_ub=b_ub,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    method="interior-point",
                )
            except Exception:
                res = None

        if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
            break

        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        sg = float(g.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(sg) or sg <= 0.0:
            break
        g = g / sg

        improved = False
        for alpha in (0.1, 0.3, 0.6, 1.0):
            cand = (1.0 - alpha) * a_pos + alpha * g
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            scs = float(cand.sum())
            if scs <= 0.0:
                continue
            cand = cand / scs
            sc = _score_fast(cand)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
                best = cand
                best_sc = sc
                improved = True

        calls += 1
        if improved:
            no_imp = 0
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp >= 4:
                break

    return best, best_sc


def _generate_starts(n, parent, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    starts = []

    def add(a):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size != n:
            return

        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            return
        a = a / s

        for old in starts:
            if old.size == n and float(np.max(np.abs(a - old))) < 1e-7:
                return
        starts.append(a)

    if parent is not None:
        p = _normalize(parent)
        if p.size > 0:
            P = p.size
            if n == P:
                add(p)
                add(p + 0.05 * max(1.0, float(np.mean(p))) * np.abs(rng.normal(size=n)))
                for pw in (1.2, 1.5, 2.0):
                    add(np.power(p, pw))
            else:
                idx = np.linspace(0.0, P - 1.0, n)
                left = np.floor(idx).astype(int)
                left = np.clip(left, 0, P - 1)
                right = np.minimum(left + 1, P - 1)
                frac = idx - left
                a = p[left] * (1.0 - frac) + p[right] * frac
                a = np.maximum(a, 1e-6)
                a += 0.03 * max(1.0, float(np.mean(a))) * np.abs(rng.normal(size=n))
                add(a)
                for pw in (1.2, 1.8):
                    add(np.power(a, pw))

    add(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    x = np.linspace(1e-4, 1.0 - 1e-4, n, dtype=np.float64)

    for g in (0.12, 0.22, 0.32, 0.45):
        f = np.power(x * (1.0 - x), -g)
        add(f)

    for _ in range(2):
        w = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.25))
        f = (
            np.exp(-((x) ** 2) / (2.0 * w * w))
            + np.exp(-((1.0 - x) ** 2) / (2.0 * w * w))
            + 0.05
        )
        add(f)

    for _ in range(2):
        c1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
        c2 = float(rng.choice([0.0, 1.0, 1.0 - c1]))
        w1 = float(rng.uniform(0.04, 0.25))
        w2 = float(rng.uniform(0.04, 0.25))
        f = (
            np.exp(-((x - c1) ** 2) / (2.0 * w1 * w1))
            + 0.8 * np.exp(-((x - c2) ** 2) / (2.0 * w2 * w2))
            + 0.05
        )
        add(f)

    for _ in range(2):
        f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for _ in range(2):
            k = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 8)))
            phi = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.55))
            f += amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * k * x + phi)
        add(f)

    add(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.6, n)))

    r = float(rng.uniform(0.55, 0.90))
    f = np.power(r, np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64))
    add(f)
    add(f[::-1])

    if not starts:
        starts = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]

    return starts


def _improve_one(a, n, stop, seed, goal):
    best = _normalize(a)
    best_sc = _score_fast(best)
    start = time.monotonic()

    if n >= 2 and time.monotonic() < stop - 10:
        t_four = min(stop - 10, max(6.0, (stop - start) * 0.25))
        cand, sc = _fourier_optimize(best, n, t_four, goal, seed)
        if cand is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = cand
            best_sc = sc

    if n <= 512 and time.monotonic() < stop - 30:
        rem = stop - time.monotonic()
        total = rem * 0.45
        betas = [int(max(50, 2 * n)), int(max(80, 10 * n))]
        t_each = total / max(1, len(betas))

        for bi, beta in enumerate(betas):
            if time.monotonic() < stop - 25:
                t_end = min(stop - 20, time.monotonic() + t_each)
                cand, sc = _lbfgs_one(best, n, t_end, beta, seed + 17 + 31 * bi)
                if cand is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best = cand
                    best_sc = sc

    if n <= 384 and time.monotonic() < stop - 15:
        t_lp = min(stop - 12, time.monotonic() + max(6.0, (stop - start) * 0.12))
        cand, sc = _lp_blend(best, n, t_lp, seed + 999)
        if cand is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = cand
            best_sc = sc

    if n >= 2 and time.monotonic() < stop - 3:
        cand, sc = _local_polish(best, n, stop - 2.0, seed + 777)
        if cand is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = cand
            best_sc = sc

    return best, best_sc


def _optimize_length(n, stop, seed, parent, goal):
    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= stop:
        return None, math.inf

    starts = _generate_starts(n, parent, seed)
    scored = []
    for a in starts:
        sc = _score_fast(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, a))

    if not scored:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return a, _score_fast(a)

    scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])

    max_starts = 4 if n <= 384 else 3
    avail = max(0.0, stop - time.monotonic())
    max_starts = max(1, min(max_starts, int(avail // 20) + 1))
    starts = [a for _, a in scored[:max_starts]]

    best = None
    best_sc = math.inf
    idx = 0

    for a in starts:
        if time.monotonic() > stop - 10:
            break

        rem = stop - time.monotonic()
        left = len(starts) - idx
        alloc = rem / max(1, left)
        if idx == 0:
            alloc = min(rem, alloc * 1.5)

        s_stop = min(stop - 5.0, time.monotonic() + alloc)
        if s_stop <= time.monotonic() + 4.0:
            break

        cand, sc = _improve_one(a, n, s_stop, seed + 131 * idx, goal)
        if cand is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = cand
            best_sc = sc

        idx += 1

    return best, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    seed_int = int(seed)

    reserve = max(5.0, min(60.0, 0.05 * budget))
    deadline = t0 + (budget - reserve)

    parent = _get_parent()

    if parent.size > 0:
        best_seq = parent.copy()
        try:
            best_score = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_seq]))
        except Exception:
            best_score = math.inf
    else:
        best_seq = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0
        try:
            best_score = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_seq]))
        except Exception:
            best_score = 2.0

    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_seq = _normalize(best_seq)
        best_score = _score_fast(best_seq)

    def consider(cand, fast_sc):
        nonlocal best_seq, best_score

        try:
            cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            return

        if cand.size == 0:
            return

        cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)

        if not math.isfinite(fast_sc) or fast_sc > best_score + 0.05:
            return

        try:
            exact = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in cand]))
        except Exception:
            exact = math.inf

        if math.isfinite(exact) and exact < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_seq = cand.copy()
            best_score = exact

    goal_score = _TARGET_BOUND
    if math.isfinite(best_score) and best_score > 0.0:
        goal_score = max(1.2, min(_TARGET_BOUND, 0.995 * best_score))

    Lp = int(best_seq.size) if best_seq.size > 1 else 0
    extra = [64, 96, 128, 160, 192, 224, 256, 288, 320, 384, 512, 768, 1024]

    if Lp > 0:
        for L in (Lp - 64, Lp + 64, Lp // 2, int(Lp * 1.5)):
            if L not in extra:
                extra.append(L)

    ordered = []
    seen = set()

    if Lp > 1:
        ordered.append(Lp)
        seen.add(Lp)

    others = [L for L in extra if 2 <= L <= 2048 and L not in seen]

    if Lp > 1:
        others.sort(key=lambda L: (abs(L - Lp), L))
    else:
        others.sort()

    for L in others:
        if L not in seen:
            seen.add(L)
            ordered.append(L)
        if len(ordered) >= 9:
            break

    if not ordered:
        ordered = [128, 192, 256]

    for L in ordered:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
            break
        if math.isfinite(best_score) and best_score < 1.49:
            break

        rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 30.0:
            break

        is_primary = (int(L) == Lp)
        if is_primary:
            alloc = min(560.0, max(80.0, 0.38 * rem))
        else:
            alloc = min(140.0, max(25.0, 0.08 * rem))

        alloc = min(alloc, rem - 30.0)
        if alloc < 20.0:
            continue

        stop = time.monotonic() + alloc
        try:
            cand, fast_sc = _optimize_length(int(L), stop, seed_int + 100003 * int(L), parent, goal_score)
        except Exception:
            cand = None
            fast_sc = math.inf

        if cand is not None:
            consider(cand, fast_sc)

        goal_score = _TARGET_BOUND
        if math.isfinite(best_score) and best_score > 0.0:
            goal_score = max(1.2, min(_TARGET_BOUND, 0.995 * best_score))

    final_stop = t0 + budget - max(10.0, 0.02 * budget)

    if best_seq.size > 1 and time.monotonic() < final_stop - 5.0:
        nb = int(best_seq.size)

        if nb <= 512 and time.monotonic() < final_stop - 15.0:
            t_end = min(final_stop - 10.0, time.monotonic() + 35.0)
            try:
                cand, sc = _lp_blend(best_seq, nb, t_end, seed_int + 123)
            except Exception:
                cand, sc = None, math.inf
            if cand is not None:
                consider(cand, sc)

        if time.monotonic() < final_stop - 4.0:
            try:
                cand, sc = _local_polish(best_seq, nb, final_stop - 3.0, seed_int + 321)
            except Exception:
                cand, sc = None, math.inf
            if cand is not None:
                consider(cand, sc)

    final_seq = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, float(x)))) for x in best_seq]

    try:
        fs = float(evaluate_sequence(final_seq))
    except Exception:
        fs = math.inf

    if not math.isfinite(fs):
        fallback = [1.0]
        try:
            fs2 = float(evaluate_sequence(fallback))
        except Exception:
            fs2 = math.inf

        if math.isfinite(fs2):
            return fallback

    return final_seq
```
